ERM-P027 — Accelerated evidence only under failure-mechanism equivalence
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P027

PROPERTY_NAME

Accelerated evidence only under failure-mechanism equivalence

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Accelerated life testing, Arrhenius/stress-life models, environmental testing, ESS and highly accelerated discovery methods.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. LIFE_DATA_AND_STATISTICAL_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

  2. HARDWARE_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

  3. DOMAIN_SPECIFIC

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. STATISTICAL_RELIABILITY_ANCESTRY

  2. RELIABILITY_ENGINEERING_NATIVE

  3. DOMAIN_TRANSLATION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Raise temperature, voltage, vibration or use rate to observe failures faster and extrapolate to field life.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Long field lives make direct tests slow, but excessive/wrong stress can activate different mechanisms or invalidate extrapolation.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Estimate or discover the field-relevant failure mechanism and life under the declared environment/profile.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Stress changes mechanism, multiple mechanisms mix, model form is wrong, specimens differ, or HALT discovery is reported as a life demonstration.

MECHANISM

Select stress from physics/mechanism; verify failure-mode equivalence; use multiple levels; validate model; separate screening/discovery from quantitative life estimation.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Known stress-accelerable degradation, long life, design comparison or mechanism discovery.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Use direct field-like stress, engineering margin or targeted destructive test when quantitative acceleration adds no decision value.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Mechanism knowledge, stress control, representative specimens, sufficient failures/censoring data, covariates and uncertainty propagation.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

Access, modularity, safe isolation, procedures, test equipment, spares, tools, skills, logistics, configuration data and time under actual field conditions.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Obtains earlier evidence without confusing non-field damage with field reliability.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Reliability engineer, design/materials owner, qualification authority and maintenance planner.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Wrong activation energy; combined stresses; threshold/nonlinear effects; sample ageing; over-extrapolation; screening damage.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Acceleration models are often selected for convenience and validated over too narrow a stress range.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Generic high-stress tests narrowed toward physics-of-failure, mechanism-confirmed acceleration and discovery-versus-demonstration separation.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A mechanism-linked acceleration case with failure-mode confirmation, model diagnostics, uncertainty and explicit non-transfer regions.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A chamber test, HALT or ESS label is not the property; decision-relevant evidence about the field mechanism is.

CURRENT_STATUS

ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH

HIGH

FORMAL_OR_PROBABILISTIC_STRENGTH

VERY_HIGH

LIFE_DATA_OR_FIELD_STRENGTH

MODERATE_TO_HIGH

DEMONSTRATION_TEST_STRENGTH

HIGH

INCIDENT_OR_FAILURE_CASE_STRENGTH

MODERATE

PROPERTY

CLAIM

POPULATION_OR_DENOMINATOR

CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY

Representative demand/workload/environment with consequence-important rare cases identified.

TEST_OR_OBSERVATION_DESIGN

Predeclared sampling/censoring, failure scoring, exposure and stopping rules.

MODEL

Model selected for the stated estimand: life distribution, recurrent-event process, binomial demand, availability state model or direct functional check.

ASSUMPTIONS

Independence, stationarity, censoring, repair effectiveness, acceleration, coverage and representativeness are explicit.

CONFIDENCE_OR_UNCERTAINTY

Intervals, posterior uncertainty or bounded qualitative confidence; point estimates alone are insufficient.

CENSORING_AND_MISSINGNESS

Right/interval censoring, unreported events, lost telemetry and excluded windows are recorded.

COVERAGE

ERM-P028 — Field-data feedback, model calibration and explicit uncertainty
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P028

PROPERTY_NAME

Field-data feedback, model calibration and explicit uncertainty

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Field returns, reliability data systems, Bayesian updating and modern telemetry/incident feedback.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. LIFE_DATA_AND_STATISTICAL_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

  2. HARDWARE_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

  3. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_OR_SRE_TRANSLATION

  4. HYBRID

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. STATISTICAL_RELIABILITY_ANCESTRY

  2. RELIABILITY_ENGINEERING_NATIVE

  3. DOMAIN_TRANSLATION

  4. HYBRID_RESOLUTION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Compare predicted part failure rates and MTBF with fleet returns and revise estimates.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Design predictions and qualification data diverge from real populations, profiles, environments, dependencies and maintenance quality.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Maintain a current evidence distribution for mission failure, recurrence, downtime and repair under field conditions.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Returns lack exposure; under-reporting; mixed configurations; warranty bias; changed profile; point estimates without intervals; incidents not linked to denominator.

MECHANISM

Collect contextualised events and non-events; preserve censoring/exposure; stratify configuration/profile; update models; retain uncertainty and contrary cases.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Fielded system, changing use/environment, prediction-dependent decisions, sparse test evidence or repeated surprise failures.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Use direct trend/stratified counts with honest uncertainty when complex Bayesian or parametric models do not improve decisions.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Telemetry/reporting, population inventory, exposure, configuration identity, failure adjudication, privacy/access and model governance.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

Access, modularity, safe isolation, procedures, test equipment, spares, tools, skills, logistics, configuration data and time under actual field conditions.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Detects model drift and unknown modes, calibrates maintenance/spares and prevents stale predictions governing current systems.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Design owner, fleet/service operator, maintainer, supplier manager and mission authority.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Missing not-at-random reports; denominator changes; fleet selection; repair resets; silent degradation; survivorship and delayed labels.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Field data are observational and confounded; frequent updating can overreact to noise or encode operational inequity.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Static handbook rates evolved toward contextual field databases, Bayesian/hierarchical models, uncertainty and telemetry-linked incident learning.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A continuously quality-checked, configuration/profile-stratified field evidence process that can falsify prior predictions and expire stale claims.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A data lake or reliability report is not the property; calibrated decision evidence with usable denominators is.

CURRENT_STATUS

RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH

TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH

HIGH_WITH_STRATIFICATION

ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY

HIGH

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

MODERATE

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. SRC-033

  2. SRC-040

  3. SRC-047

  4. SRC-051

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. SRC-058

  2. SRC-059

  3. SRC-060

  4. SRC-073

EMPIRICAL_OR_FIELD_EVIDENCE

Current incident evidence repeatedly exposes untested scale, dependencies and support conditions absent from design predictions (SRC-058–SRC-062).

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

Field data can be less informative than controlled tests for rare mechanisms when exposure and reporting are poor; hybrid evidence is needed.

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How should hierarchical transfer borrow strength without masking site/configuration-specific failure mechanisms?

ERM-P029 — Reliability evidence bound to current configuration and change
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P029

PROPERTY_NAME

Reliability evidence bound to current configuration and change

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Configuration control in military reliability, qualification by item identity, software release/change control and modern deployment reliability.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. HARDWARE_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

  2. SOFTWARE_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

  3. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_OR_SRE_TRANSLATION

  4. CONVERGENT_ENGINEERING

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. RELIABILITY_ENGINEERING_NATIVE

  2. SYSTEMS_ENGINEERING_IMPORT_OR_SHARED_ANCESTRY

  3. DOMAIN_TRANSLATION

  4. CONVERGENT_PROPERTY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Apply test/prediction results to the tested part number, drawing revision and environment.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Evidence silently transfers after component, software, model, data, supplier, dependency, procedure or support organisation changes.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

The reliability claim remains valid for the exact deployed configuration and its current profile/dependencies.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Stale qualification; partial rollout; hidden supplier substitution; model/data update; configuration drift; recovery artefact incompatible; mixed fleet denominator.

MECHANISM

Record evidence-to-configuration lineage; assess change impact; invalidate/retest affected claims; stage/canary changes; monitor early field evidence.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Any material design, software, data/model, supplier, infrastructure, maintenance-procedure or support change.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For a small reversible change, run a targeted functional/regression check and explicitly narrow the inherited claim.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Unique identities, bill/configuration/dependency inventory, change detection, evidence links, rollout state and rollback/recovery artefacts.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Stops old evidence from licensing a materially new system and supports targeted rather than wholesale retesting.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Release/change authority, reliability owner, operator, supplier manager and acceptance authority.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Version ambiguity; mutable dependency; untracked feature flag; rollback incompatibility; staged population mixed in one metric; undocumented field repair.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Overly strict binding can block useful evidence transfer and slow iteration; impact analysis must be proportional.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Drawing/part qualification evolved toward end-to-end software/data/model/dependency and operational-organisation evidence lineage.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A live claim-evidence-configuration graph with explicit inheritance rationale, invalidation triggers, targeted checks and field confirmation.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A configuration-management database is not the property; knowing which evidence still applies is.

EMPIRICAL_OR_FIELD_EVIDENCE

The CrowdStrike event was tied to a specific content update and affected only hosts receiving it in a time window, illustrating configuration/exposure identity (SRC-061, SRC-062).

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

Some robust mechanisms transfer across versions; transfer must be argued, not prohibited by default.

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How can continuously changing dependencies be fingerprinted cheaply enough for real-time evidence validity?

ERM-P030 — Software reliability under an operational input profile
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P030

PROPERTY_NAME

Software reliability under an operational input profile

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Jelinski–Moranda and Goel–Okumoto growth models; Musa operational profiles; field software failure studies.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. SOFTWARE_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

  2. LIFE_DATA_AND_STATISTICAL_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

  3. HYBRID

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. STATISTICAL_RELIABILITY_ANCESTRY

  2. DOMAIN_TRANSLATION

  3. HYBRID_RESOLUTION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Estimate software failure intensity as faults are found and removed during testing.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Software does not wear out like hardware; failures require fault activation by inputs/state, and fixes/releases alter the population and profile.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Probability/intensity of software-caused functional failure for the current version under the declared input/state/workload profile.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Fault-content model treated as literal; perfect fix assumed; test inputs unrepresentative; interactions/dependencies omitted; version changes invalidate growth curve.

MECHANISM

Use operational-profile testing, failure-severity/state classification, version binding, model comparison, fix-effect evidence and post-release monitoring.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Software-dominated function with enough repeated operations/failures for an estimable question and a stable release/profile window.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Use targeted functional, property, stress and regression tests when a numerical software failure-rate model will not change the decision.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Current version/configuration, input/state distribution, failure oracle, exposure, fix history and dependency environment.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Avoids importing wear-out assumptions while preserving probabilistic evidence for activated faults.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Software/reliability engineer, release authority, service owner and mission owner.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Perfect debugging; independent faults; stable profile; common-mode dependency; rare catastrophic paths; test oracle failure.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Many growth models fit historical curves similarly yet extrapolate poorly; model selection after seeing data invites overfit.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Fault-count curves evolved toward operational-profile, severity, state, architecture/dependency and change-aware evidence.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A version- and profile-bound software functional-failure claim triangulated by targeted tests, field exposure and uncertainty—not a generic “software MTBF.”

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A software reliability growth chart is optional; representative evidence about the current executable behaviour is.

CURRENT_STATUS

DOMAIN_SPECIFIC

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH

HIGH

FORMAL_OR_PROBABILISTIC_STRENGTH

VERY_HIGH

LIFE_DATA_OR_FIELD_STRENGTH

MIXED

DEMONSTRATION_TEST_STRENGTH

HIGH

INCIDENT_OR_FAILURE_CASE_STRENGTH

HIGH

OPERATIONAL_PRACTICE_STRENGTH

MODERATE

REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH

MODERATE

TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH

LOW_TO_MODERATE

ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY

VERY_HIGH

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

HIGH

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. SRC-017

  2. SRC-018

  3. SRC-019

  4. SRC-080

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. SRC-042

  2. SRC-051

  3. SRC-081

  4. SRC-083

EMPIRICAL_OR_FIELD_EVIDENCE

Internet-service studies and outages show configuration, operations and dependencies are often co-causes rather than separable “software defects” (SRC-058–SRC-062, SRC-081).

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

For stable narrow programs with controlled profiles, SRGMs can support planning; transfer beyond those assumptions is weak.

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How should software reliability evidence account for adaptive configuration and continuously deployed code without meaningless averaging?

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE

Representative demand/workload/environment with consequence-important rare cases identified.

TEST_OR_OBSERVATION_DESIGN

Predeclared sampling/censoring, failure scoring, exposure and stopping rules.

MODEL

Model selected for the stated estimand: life distribution, recurrent-event process, binomial demand, availability state model or direct functional check.

ASSUMPTIONS

Independence, stationarity, censoring, repair effectiveness, acceleration, coverage and representativeness are explicit.

CONFIDENCE_OR_UNCERTAINTY

Intervals, posterior uncertainty or bounded qualitative confidence; point estimates alone are insufficient.

CENSORING_AND_MISSINGNESS

Right/interval censoring, unreported events, lost telemetry and excluded windows are recorded.

COVERAGE

Claim coverage is limited to tested/observed failure modes, environments and paths.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Software/reliability engineer, release authority, service owner and mission owner.

CHEAP_PATH

Use targeted functional, property, stress and regression tests when a numerical software failure-rate model will not change the decision.

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

For stable narrow programs with controlled profiles, SRGMs can support planning; transfer beyond those assumptions is weak.

EXPIRY_OR_FRESHNESS

Evidence expires when configuration, profile, environment, support organisation or dependencies materially change.

MATURE_FORM

A version- and profile-bound software functional-failure claim triangulated by targeted tests, field exposure and uncertainty—not a generic “software MTBF.”

DISPOSITION_RATIONALE

Admitted or rejected according to mechanism, trigger, assumption validity, evidence and anti-ceremony analysis.

ERM-P031 — User-journey and consumer-consequence service reliability
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P031

PROPERTY_NAME

User-journey and consumer-consequence service reliability

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

High-availability service engineering, SRE SLIs/SLOs and end-to-end operational monitoring.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_OR_SRE_TRANSLATION

  2. SOFTWARE_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

  3. CONVERGENT_ENGINEERING

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_TRANSLATION

  2. RELIABILITY_ENGINEERING_NATIVE

  3. CONVERGENT_PROPERTY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Measure request success/latency and service uptime.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Component or aggregate request metrics can remain green while critical multi-step journeys, user classes, freshness, durability or partial service fail.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Named consumer journey achieves correct, timely, sufficiently fresh/durable outcome over the relevant window and dependencies.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Request-weighted average hides a small user cohort, tail latency, dependency path, stale data, partial outage or repeated short disruptions.

MECHANISM

Define journey-level outcomes; stratify consumers and consequence; include tails/freshness/durability; measure dependency and degraded-state success.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Externally consumed software/service whose important outcomes span components, dependencies or multiple steps.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For simple single-step services, one end-to-end functional probe plus incident review may be sufficient.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Consumer definitions, telemetry across journey, privacy-preserving correlation, state correctness, dependency observability and denominator governance.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Enumerated fault/failure population, observable signals, calibrated thresholds, isolation resolution, false-alarm and missed-detection costs.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Aligns service reliability decisions with actual utility rather than local uptime.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

End users, mission/business owner, service owner and on-call operator.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Synthetic probe differs from real user; missing client-side failures; aggregation; bot/request volume dominates; rare critical journey underweighted.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

User happiness is multidimensional and difficult to reduce to a ratio; measurement itself can be incomplete or inequitable.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Machine/process uptime evolved toward request outcomes, tails, user journeys, cohorts, freshness and consequence.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A small set of end-to-end, cohort-aware, consequence-linked measures with diagnostic drill-down and explicit blind spots.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A dashboard or “golden signals” panel is not the property; current evidence that consumers receive required outcomes is.

Meta’s DNS servers were locally operational but users could not reach services; GitLab separated code-repository integrity from database data loss (SRC-059, SRC-060).

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

No single user-journey metric covers all consequences; qualitative incident evidence and targeted tests remain necessary.

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How should reliability protect low-volume high-consequence consumers without allowing arbitrary metric proliferation?

APPLICABLE

true

PROPERTY

User-journey and consumer-consequence service reliability

INITIATING_FAULT_OR_FAILURE

Request-weighted average hides a small user cohort, tail latency, dependency path, stale data, partial outage or repeated short disruptions.

LOCAL_EFFECT

Loss, corruption, delay, false indication or degraded performance at the initiating item.

NEXT_LEVEL_EFFECT

Effect on the enclosing function, dependency path or maintenance process.

END_EFFECT

Request-weighted average hides a small user cohort, tail latency, dependency path, stale data, partial outage or repeated short disruptions.

PROPAGATION_PATH

Functional, physical, state, timing, control, resource or organisational coupling.

CONTAINMENT_BOUNDARY

A declared fault-containment region or explicit stop/degradation boundary.

DEPENDENCIES

Consumer definitions, telemetry across journey, privacy-preserving correlation, state correctness, dependency observability and denominator governance.

LATENT_CONDITION

Standby, monitoring, repair or dependency defects may remain latent until demand.

CONTROL

Define journey-level outcomes; stratify consumers and consequence; include tails/freshness/durability; measure dependency and degraded-state success.

Unmodelled combinations, dependence, coverage gaps and changed configuration remain.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PROFILE
APPLICABLE

true

PROPERTY

User-journey and consumer-consequence service reliability

TARGET_FAULTS_FAILURES

Request-weighted average hides a small user cohort, tail latency, dependency path, stale data, partial outage or repeated short disruptions.

OBSERVABLES

Function outputs, timing, state, residuals, dependency signals and user/mission outcomes.

DETECTION_COVERAGE

Coverage is stated for an enumerated fault/failure population, operating mode and latency window.

FALSE_ALARM_COST

Unnecessary maintenance, failover, shutdown, operator load or customer disruption.

MISSED_DETECTION_COST

Continued propagation, latent damage, missed recovery window or false assurance.

ISOLATION_RESOLUTION

Replaceable unit, functional region, dependency or ambiguity group appropriate to the action.

AMBIGUITY_GROUPS

Plausible causes producing the same symptom are preserved until discriminated.

LATENCY

Detection and isolation time are bounded relative to propagation and recovery deadlines.

CONFIDENCE_CALIBRATION

Uncertainty, thresholds, drift and out-of-distribution conditions are exposed.

OPERATOR_OR_AUTOMATION_ACTION

Action is authority-bounded, reversible where feasible and matched to diagnosis confidence.

RECOVERY_COVERAGE

A detected fault counts only if the prescribed action restores or contains the target failure with known coverage.

MATURE_FORM

A small set of end-to-end, cohort-aware, consequence-linked measures with diagnostic drill-down and explicit blind spots.

ERM-P032 — SLI/SLO and error-budget governance as a scoped decision aid
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P032

PROPERTY_NAME

SLI/SLO and error-budget governance as a scoped decision aid

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Google SRE practice and subsequent industrial adoption/critique.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_OR_SRE_TRANSLATION

  2. DOMAIN_SPECIFIC

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_TRANSLATION

  2. DOMAIN_TRANSLATION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Set an SLO below 100%, measure bad events, and spend the error budget to balance reliability and change velocity.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Teams need an operational threshold linking observed service outcomes to release, stabilisation and alert decisions.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

A user/mission-relevant outcome remains within an agreed loss budget over a declared denominator/window, with action when exhausted.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Easy metric chosen; time/event denominator mismatch; cohort/tail hidden; maintenance excluded; budget treated as permission; chronic exhaustion creates nonsense.

MECHANISM

Select user-relevant SLI; document denominator/window/exclusions; use burn-rate/tail/cohort views; preagree actions; review validity and gaming.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Repeatable online service with measurable consumer outcomes and cross-functional authority to act on budget state.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Use direct incident/change judgement for small or low-volume services where a stable ratio is misleading.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Reliable telemetry, user-journey mapping, stakeholder agreement, release authority, dependency awareness and review cadence.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Creates a common, current decision signal and makes reliability/change trade-offs explicit.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Service owner, product/change authority, on-call and users represented by the SLI.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Nine-count branding; denominator manipulation; omitted cohorts; short/long outage equivalence; chronic debt; threshold chosen backwards from current performance.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Boolean ratios fit request/response better than many other services; correct error-budget response and alert thresholds remain contested.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Uptime targets evolved toward user-focused SLIs, multiwindow burn rates, policy actions and explicit critique of denominator/threshold assumptions.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A revisable, consumer-grounded governance instrument used alongside incident, dependency and tail evidence—not a universal reliability score.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

The SLO dashboard and error-budget ritual are optional; the property is a valid current decision boundary and agreed response.

CURRENT_STATUS

USEFUL_BUT_EASILY_GAMED

EMPIRICAL_OR_FIELD_EVIDENCE

SRE practice supplies detailed operational mechanisms, while practitioner critiques identify denominator, threshold and chronic-budget failure modes (SRC-063–SRC-067).

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

No robust multi-organisation evidence establishes one SLO formula or error-budget policy as universally optimal.

OPEN_QUESTIONS

What empirical decision rules best respond to budget exhaustion across different service archetypes and consequence distributions?

ERM-P033 — End-to-end health checks and symptom-versus-cause separation
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P033

PROPERTY_NAME

End-to-end health checks and symptom-versus-cause separation

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Built-in test/liveness checks, model-based diagnosis and SRE symptom-oriented monitoring.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. DIAGNOSTICS_AND_PHM_LINEAGE

  2. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_OR_SRE_TRANSLATION

  3. HYBRID

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. MAINTAINABILITY_NATIVE

  2. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_TRANSLATION

  3. HYBRID_RESOLUTION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Poll component liveness and page on unhealthy status.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

A component heartbeat can be green while the actual path is unusable; conversely a symptom can identify impact without establishing cause.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Detect end-to-end functional loss quickly while preserving a separate evidence path for causal diagnosis.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Shallow probe; cached/static response; monitor bypasses dependency/state/authentication; probe shares failure; symptom automatically labelled root cause.

MECHANISM

Use layered probes: end-to-end outcome for impact, component/dependency signals for diagnosis; test probe coverage/failure; validate after recovery.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Remote automated service, failover, load balancer health, readiness/liveness, synthetic transactions or auto-remediation.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

A direct user-visible transaction is often cheaper and stronger than many internal health bits.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Representative journey, safe test account/data, independent monitoring path, timing and known blind spots.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Enumerated fault/failure population, observable signals, calibrated thresholds, isolation resolution, false-alarm and missed-detection costs.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Reduces false-green availability and shortens diagnosis without confusing symptom with mechanism.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

On-call operator, automated traffic manager, incident commander and users.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Probe not representative; synthetic succeeds while cohort fails; health endpoint isolated from data plane; monitor unavailable; causal overreach.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Deep probes can be costly, create load or mutate state; they cannot cover all semantic outcomes.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Single component heartbeat evolved toward layered end-to-end functional monitoring, cohort telemetry and dependency diagnosis.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A documented coverage stack: user/mission outcome detection, diagnostic signals, independent probe health and post-recovery validation.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A green checkmark is not the property; observed required function plus understood coverage is.

CURRENT_STATUS

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PROPERTY

Meta’s DNS servers were operational but unreachable, a direct example of a locally green component and failed service path (SRC-059).

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PROFILE
APPLICABLE

true

PROPERTY

End-to-end health checks and symptom-versus-cause separation

TARGET_FAULTS_FAILURES

Shallow probe; cached/static response; monitor bypasses dependency/state/authentication; probe shares failure; symptom automatically labelled root cause.

OBSERVABLES

Function outputs, timing, state, residuals, dependency signals and user/mission outcomes.

DETECTION_COVERAGE

Coverage is stated for an enumerated fault/failure population, operating mode and latency window.

FALSE_ALARM_COST

Unnecessary maintenance, failover, shutdown, operator load or customer disruption.

MISSED_DETECTION_COST

Continued propagation, latent damage, missed recovery window or false assurance.

ISOLATION_RESOLUTION

Replaceable unit, functional region, dependency or ambiguity group appropriate to the action.

AMBIGUITY_GROUPS

Plausible causes producing the same symptom are preserved until discriminated.

LATENCY

Detection and isolation time are bounded relative to propagation and recovery deadlines.

CONFIDENCE_CALIBRATION

Uncertainty, thresholds, drift and out-of-distribution conditions are exposed.

OPERATOR_OR_AUTOMATION_ACTION

Action is authority-bounded, reversible where feasible and matched to diagnosis confidence.

RECOVERY_COVERAGE

A detected fault counts only if the prescribed action restores or contains the target failure with known coverage.

MATURE_FORM

A documented coverage stack: user/mission outcome detection, diagnostic signals, independent probe health and post-recovery validation.

false

ERM-P034 — Explicit dependency, correlation and hidden single-point mapping
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P034

PROPERTY_NAME

Explicit dependency, correlation and hidden single-point mapping

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

RBD/FTA serial structures, supply/logistics analysis and modern cloud/service dependency mapping.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. FAILURE_ANALYSIS_LINEAGE

  2. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_OR_SRE_TRANSLATION

  3. CONVERGENT_ENGINEERING

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. RELIABILITY_ENGINEERING_NATIVE

  2. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_TRANSLATION

  3. SYSTEMS_ENGINEERING_IMPORT_OR_SHARED_ANCESTRY

  4. CONVERGENT_PROPERTY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Represent series/parallel component dependencies in an RBD.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Architecture diagrams omit shared DNS, identity, control planes, libraries, regions, suppliers, spares, people or change channels that dominate failure.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Every dependency necessary for the critical function or its recovery is identified with consequence, alternatives and correlation.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Unknown transitive dependency; fallback uses same provider/control; SLA substitutes for observed performance; human/on-call or logistics bottleneck omitted.

MECHANISM

Discover runtime and organisational dependencies; classify criticality/failure domains; test fallback; record correlated change/maintenance; monitor supplier performance.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

End-to-end service, outsourced/supplier component, multi-region design, recovery plan, shared platform or long logistics chain.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For simple systems, trace one critical user/mission path and its recovery path rather than build a universal service map.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Telemetry/tracing, contracts plus observed data, architecture and support knowledge, ownership and change notifications.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

Credible failure-domain independence, common-cause analysis, standby condition, switchover coverage and state compatibility.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Exposes serial reliability and common-cause risks that component metrics and nominal redundancy hide.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Architect, service/fleet owner, supplier manager, operator, continuity planner and mission owner.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Dynamic discovery gaps; shadow dependency; circular fallback; shared credentials; control-plane dependence; concentrated maintainer/supplier.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Maps go stale quickly and can become inventory ceremony; causal dependence is richer than static edges.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Static blocks evolved toward dynamic, transitive, control/state/human/supply dependency and correlated-change models.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A decision-focused dependency model refreshed by telemetry and incidents, including recovery dependencies and credible fallback evidence.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A service map is not the property; knowing which shared dependency can defeat required function and what is done about it is.

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

Dependency elimination is impossible; some shared platforms improve reliability through expertise and scale, so the property is visibility and evidence, not universal decentralisation.

INDEPENDENCE_EVIDENCE

COMMON_CAUSE_COUPLINGS

STANDBY_LATENCY_OR_DORMANCY

ERM-P035 — Hypothesis-bound fault injection and chaos experimentation
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P035

PROPERTY_NAME

Hypothesis-bound fault injection and chaos experimentation

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Hardware/software fault injection, dependability validation and 2010s chaos engineering.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. FAULT_INJECTION_OR_CHAOS_TRANSLATION

  2. FAULT_TOLERANT_COMPUTING_LINEAGE

  3. DOMAIN_SPECIFIC

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. FAULT_TOLERANCE_IMPORT_OR_SHARED_ANCESTRY

  2. DOMAIN_TRANSLATION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Inject component or service failures to see whether redundancy/recovery works.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Analytical claims about detection, containment, degradation and recovery can remain untested in the integrated current system.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Discriminate a live reliability uncertainty for a specified fault, profile, boundary and decision without causing disproportionate harm.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Random breakage; nonrepresentative fault; weak oracle; no decision consumer; production risk exceeds value; repeated ritual after claim already established.

MECHANISM

State hypothesis/steady-state metric; choose representative fault and blast radius; predefine abort; observe full recovery/postconditions; record decision/update.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Material integrated uncertainty not cheaply resolved by static analysis, simulation, staging or direct functional test.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Use unit/integration test, simulation, replay or nonproduction exercise when it resolves the same uncertainty more safely/cheaply.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Containment, observability, recovery readiness, representative load/state, authorisation, abort/fallback and post-experiment analysis.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Finds integration/coverage gaps and supplies current empirical evidence for specific resilience claims.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Service/mission owner, operator, reliability engineer and change authority.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Toy fault; survivorship; experiment itself causes outage; only steady-state metric checked; no state validation; outcomes not acted upon.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Industrial evidence is advocacy-heavy; systematic review still finds evaluation and adoption gaps; production experimentation creates ethical/operational risk.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Fault injection evolved from component coverage testing toward hypothesis-driven system experiments with blast-radius and decision controls.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A sparse portfolio of claim-bound experiments chosen by expected information value and closed through recovery validation and engineering action.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

“Chaos day,” monkey tooling or periodic failure ritual is not the property; decision-relevant falsification is.

CURRENT_STATUS

  1. SRC-068

  3. SRC-070

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. SRC-058

  2. SRC-059

  3. SRC-066

EMPIRICAL_OR_FIELD_EVIDENCE

Meta’s long-running storm drills aided staged restoration but had never simulated global-backbone loss, illustrating both value and coverage limits (SRC-059).

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

A successful experiment proves only the tested conditions; it never establishes general resilience.

OPEN_QUESTIONS

What empirical designs best estimate transfer from controlled experiments to rare correlated production failures?

CLAIM

A sparse portfolio of claim-bound experiments chosen by expected information value and closed through recovery validation and engineering action.

POPULATION_OR_DENOMINATOR

Declared units, missions, demands, requests, users, time windows or recurrent-event process.

CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY

Exact hardware, software, data, model, dependency and procedure versions.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE

Representative demand/workload/environment with consequence-important rare cases identified.

TEST_OR_OBSERVATION_DESIGN

MODEL

ERM-P036 — Overload-aware recovery: capacity margin, load shedding and bounded retries
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P036

PROPERTY_NAME

Overload-aware recovery: capacity margin, load shedding and bounded retries

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Queueing/capacity practice, high-availability service recovery and overload-control patterns.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_OR_SRE_TRANSLATION

  2. AVAILABILITY_AND_REPAIRABLE_SYSTEMS_LINEAGE

  3. HYBRID

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_TRANSLATION

  2. RELIABILITY_ENGINEERING_NATIVE

  3. HYBRID_RESOLUTION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Keep spare capacity, shed low-priority load and retry failed requests.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Failure removes capacity while backlog/retries increase demand, so failover/restart can trigger a second collapse.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Protect priority function and recover without unstable positive feedback or exhaustion of the remaining fault-tolerant capacity.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Retry storm; thundering herd; failover target underprovisioned; cache cold start; load shedding removes critical work; no admission control.

MECHANISM

Reserve margin; classify load; cap/constrain retries; exponential backoff/jitter; circuit break; shed by priority; ramp recovery; test cold/failed capacity.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Elastic/queued services, failover, regional loss, dependency slowdown, mass restart or bursty demand.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Reject clearly and early rather than queue/retry when capacity is exhausted and work is safely repeatable later.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Demand/capacity observability, priority semantics, idempotency, queue limits, fallback and consumer communication.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

A valid recovery source, authorised action, state/data reconciliation, dependency readiness and independently checked return-to-service postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Prevents recovery mechanisms from amplifying the initiating fault and preserves critical service.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Operator, service owner, priority users and incident commander.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Incorrect priorities; hidden downstream bottleneck; stale capacity estimate; retries across many clients synchronise; shed work not recoverable.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Spare capacity costs money/energy; load shedding can externalise harm and obscure underinvestment.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Static N+1 margin evolved toward dynamic admission, priority shedding, retry budgets and staged reconstitution.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A measured stability envelope and failure-mode-specific overload policy, including consumer consequences and recovery postconditions.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A circuit-breaker library or autoscaler is not the property; stable protected operation under the target loss is.

CURRENT_STATUS

CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH

MODERATE

FORMAL_OR_PROBABILISTIC_STRENGTH

HIGH

LIFE_DATA_OR_FIELD_STRENGTH

HIGH

MODERATE

INCIDENT_OR_FAILURE_CASE_STRENGTH

VERY_HIGH

OPERATIONAL_PRACTICE_STRENGTH

HIGH

LOW_TO_MODERATE

TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH

CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY

HIGH

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

MODERATE

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. SRC-058

  2. SRC-059

  3. SRC-063

  4. SRC-083

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. SRC-066

  2. SRC-081

EMPIRICAL_OR_FIELD_EVIDENCE

AWS reported backlogs after S3 recovery; Meta explicitly avoided turning all services on at once because caches, power and systems could crash (SRC-058, SRC-059).

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

For non-queued fixed-demand systems this property may not trigger; extra mechanisms can add complexity.

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How should retry/load-shed policies coordinate across independently owned clients and dependencies?

Reserve margin; classify load; cap/constrain retries; exponential backoff/jitter; circuit break; shed by priority; ramp recovery; test cold/failed capacity.

RESIDUAL_RISK

Unmodelled combinations, dependence, coverage gaps and changed configuration remain.

false

false

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PROFILE

RECOVERY_RESTORATION_PROFILE
APPLICABLE

true

PROPERTY

Overload-aware recovery: capacity margin, load shedding and bounded retries

INITIATING_FAILURE

Retry storm; thundering herd; failover target underprovisioned; cache cold start; load shedding removes critical work; no admission control.

RECOVERY_ACTION

Reserve margin; classify load; cap/constrain retries; exponential backoff/jitter; circuit break; shed by priority; ramp recovery; test cold/failed capacity.

AUTHORITY_SOURCE

The designated current configuration/state authority and authorised operator or controller.

CHECKPOINT_BACKUP_SPARE_OR_ALTERNATE

Declared checkpoint, replica, backup, spare, rebuild source or manual workaround.

RPO_OR_STATE_LOSS_BOUND

Explicit allowed state/data loss or “none” for state-continuous functions.

RTO_OR_RESTORATION_BOUND

Explicit time-to-restoration bound including diagnosis, logistics, repair and validation.

STATE_RECONCILIATION

Resolve divergent, stale, partial or replayed state before authoritative service resumes.

FUNCTIONAL_POSTCONDITIONS

Protect priority function and recover without unstable positive feedback or exhaustion of the remaining fault-tolerant capacity.

DATA_STATE_POSTCONDITIONS

Integrity, completeness, freshness, ordering and authority appropriate to the mission.

DEPENDENCY_POSTCONDITIONS

Critical dependencies and capacity are verified rather than assumed from local restart.

VALIDATION

Independent end-to-end functional and state checks under representative demand.

RETURN_TO_SERVICE_AUTHORITY

Named human or automated authority with explicit acceptance criteria.

ROLLBACK_OR_FALLBACK

A bounded fallback when restoration validation fails or the original cause persists.

MATURE_FORM

A measured stability envelope and failure-mode-specific overload policy, including consumer consequences and recovery postconditions.

ERM-P037 — Observability and evidence preservation during failure and recovery
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P037

PROPERTY_NAME

Observability and evidence preservation during failure and recovery

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Built-in test/maintenance records, incident logging, flight/industrial recorders and service observability.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. DIAGNOSTICS_AND_PHM_LINEAGE

  2. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_OR_SRE_TRANSLATION

  3. MAINTAINABILITY_LINEAGE

  4. HYBRID

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. MAINTAINABILITY_NATIVE

  2. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_TRANSLATION

  3. CONVERGENT_PROPERTY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Log faults and maintenance actions for troubleshooting and FRACAS.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

The initiating failure or automatic remediation destroys/overwrites telemetry, tools, clocks or state needed to diagnose, validate recovery and prevent recurrence.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Retain sufficient trustworthy evidence to determine impact, state, likely causes, actions and post-recovery correctness within time/privacy constraints.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Logs share failed control plane; clocks diverge; restart erases state; auto-remediation loops; high volume drops key evidence; recovery contaminates artefacts.

MECHANISM

Independent/remote telemetry paths; durable event/action records; time/identity correlation; bounded snapshots; evidence-preserving recovery mode; privacy/access governance.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Complex automated systems, correlated outages, remote repair, stateful recovery or rare high-consequence events.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For simple deterministic faults, record the minimal causal/action evidence rather than instrument everything.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Independent power/network/storage where warranted, data quality, clocks, access, retention, privacy and incident procedures.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Enumerated fault/failure population, observable signals, calibrated thresholds, isolation resolution, false-alarm and missed-detection costs.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

A valid recovery source, authorised action, state/data reconciliation, dependency readiness and independently checked return-to-service postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Shortens diagnosis, supports state reconciliation and allows corrective-action effectiveness to be tested.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Operator, maintainer, incident investigator, design owner and return-to-service authority.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Telemetry blind spot; observer overload; misleading stale metrics; data loss; sensitive evidence withheld; logs treated as ground truth.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Observability can be costly, intrusive and security/privacy-sensitive; more data does not guarantee diagnosis.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Fault codes evolved toward distributed traces, event/state provenance, recovery journals and independent evidence channels.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Decision-focused observability resilient to the target failure, with known coverage, integrity, retention and evidence-preserving automation.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A logging stack is not the property; reliable evidence for live decisions and recurrence learning is.

true

PROPERTY

Observability and evidence preservation during failure and recovery

NEXT_LEVEL_EFFECT

Effect on the enclosing function, dependency path or maintenance process.

END_EFFECT

Logs share failed control plane; clocks diverge; restart erases state; auto-remediation loops; high volume drops key evidence; recovery contaminates artefacts.

PROPAGATION_PATH

Functional, physical, state, timing, control, resource or organisational coupling.

CONTAINMENT_BOUNDARY

A declared fault-containment region or explicit stop/degradation boundary.

DEPENDENCIES

Independent power/network/storage where warranted, data quality, clocks, access, retention, privacy and incident procedures.

LATENCY

CONFIDENCE_CALIBRATION

OPERATOR_OR_AUTOMATION_ACTION

RECOVERY_COVERAGE

ERM-P038 — Proportional cheap path and deterministic functional check
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P038

PROPERTY_NAME

Proportional cheap path and deterministic functional check

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Tailoring in military/NASA R&M, objectives-based dependability management and decision-focused testing.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. CONVERGENT_ENGINEERING

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. RELIABILITY_ENGINEERING_NATIVE

  2. CONVERGENT_PROPERTY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Tailor the reliability programme to mission class and risk.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Full FMEA, probabilistic model, demonstration, SLO or chaos programme can cost more than the uncertainty it resolves and become ceremony.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Obtain enough trustworthy evidence for the actual decision at proportionate cost and risk.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Mandatory artefact regardless of consequence; model precision without decision sensitivity; repeated test after result cannot change action; analysis delays simple fix.

MECHANISM

State decision/uncertainty/consequence; ask whether a direct functional check, conservative bound, simple design change or acceptance is sufficient; escalate only when needed.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Every proposed reliability activity; especially low-consequence, reversible, small-scope or already-observable work.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

This property is the cheap path: simplest evidence/mechanism that resolves the live decision while preserving critical assumptions.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Clear decision consumer, consequence, reversibility, uncertainty and authority to tailor.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Preserves engineering attention for material failure risks and resists reliability bureaucracy.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Decision owner, mission/service owner and engineering team.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Under-analysis disguised as agility; consequence underestimated; direct check misses latent/tail risk; tailoring used to avoid inconvenient evidence.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Proportionality is judgement-sensitive and can rationalise weak assurance; explicit rationale and triggers for escalation are needed.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Prescriptive activity lists increasingly coexist with objectives-based, tailored, decision-relevant R&M practice.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A documented minimum-sufficient path with escalation criteria, not a blanket waiver or blanket process.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

No named artefact is privileged; only the evidence/mechanism needed for the decision survives.

ERM-P039 — Retirement of stale controls, metrics, support and reliability claims
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P039

PROPERTY_NAME

Retirement of stale controls, metrics, support and reliability claims

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Configuration/obsolescence management, field-data refresh and metric-governance practice.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. MAINTAINABILITY_LINEAGE

  2. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_OR_SRE_TRANSLATION

  3. CONVERGENT_ENGINEERING

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. MAINTAINABILITY_NATIVE

  2. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_TRANSLATION

  3. CONVERGENT_PROPERTY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Review reliability predictions, maintenance plans and support resources periodically.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Controls, thresholds, spares, procedures, models and dashboards persist after configuration/profile/consumer change and create false assurance or burden.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Only current, decision-relevant reliability controls and support capabilities govern the current system.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Obsolete spare/tool; unsupported dependency; stale profile; dead alert; inherited “nine”; old backup format; handbook rate for changed technology.

MECHANISM

Attach owner, scope, evidence, configuration, review/expiry and retirement trigger; monitor obsolescence; remove or replace controls that no longer discriminate risk.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Technology/configuration/profile/consumer change, repeated false alarms, unused metric, support deprecation or expired evidence.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Delete a stale metric/control when no consumer or decision can be named; retain a simple direct check if still useful.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Inventory and ownership, configuration/dependency change signals, evidence lineage and authority to retire contractual/process residue.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

Access, modularity, safe isolation, procedures, test equipment, spares, tools, skills, logistics, configuration data and time under actual field conditions.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Reduces false confidence, alert/maintenance burden and unsupported recovery dependence.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

System/service owner, maintainer, operator, procurement and assurance authority.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Zombie dashboard; contract inertia; metric tied to bonus; unsupported library/part; retirement removes latent protection; no replacement evidence.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Frequent change can erode comparability and institutional memory; retirement needs archival trace and consequence check.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Periodic document review broadened to live evidence freshness, control efficacy, dependency support and obsolescence risk.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Every material reliability control has a current consumer, evidence basis, configuration scope, expiry and safe retirement/replacement path.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Keeping a metric because it exists is ceremony; deliberate retention or retirement is the property.

The MIL-HDBK-217 critique shows how outdated data/models can persist institutionally after technology changes (SRC-047).

MISSION_RELIABILITY_PROFILE
APPLICABLE

true

PROPERTY

ENVIRONMENT

FAILURE_DEFINITION

Obsolete spare/tool; unsupported dependency; stale profile; dead alert; inherited “nine”; old backup format; handbook rate for changed technology.

MISSION_TIME_OR_DEMAND

ERM-P040 — Design diversity as conditional common-mode mitigation
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P040

PROPERTY_NAME

Design diversity as conditional common-mode mitigation

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

N-version programming, diverse hardware/software channels and common-cause mitigation.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. FAULT_TOLERANT_COMPUTING_LINEAGE

  2. SOFTWARE_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

  3. DOMAIN_SPECIFIC

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. FAULT_TOLERANCE_IMPORT_OR_SHARED_ANCESTRY

  2. DOMAIN_TRANSLATION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Develop multiple independent versions and vote to tolerate design faults.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Identical redundancy shares systematic faults; diversity may reduce common modes but independence is not guaranteed.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

At least one/adjudicated implementation remains correct for the target input/fault class without shared specification/model/tool failure.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Common specification ambiguity; difficult inputs induce similar errors; shared algorithm/library/data/model; voter/acceptance test wrong; diversity overwhelms maintenance.

MECHANISM

Diversify selected causal dimensions; analyse shared assumptions; use independent evidence/teams/tools where valuable; test coincident errors; preserve diagnosability and update compatibility.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Systematic design/software/model failure dominates and consequence justifies multiple implementations.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Prefer one simple implementation plus strong specification/tests when diversity cost and shared assumptions erase benefit.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Clear specification/oracle, adjudication, independent causal pathways, configuration control, joint-failure evidence and maintenance capacity.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

Credible failure-domain independence, common-cause analysis, standby condition, switchover coverage and state compatibility.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Can reduce some common design faults that identical replicas cannot tolerate.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Architect, mission authority, software/reliability owner and maintainer.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Correlated mistakes; shared training data/model; specification error; voter failure; patch divergence; extra interfaces and operational burden.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Knight–Leveson and Eckhardt–Lee directly undermine naïve independence; “different code” is not “independent failure.”

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

N-version slogans narrowed toward targeted causal diversity with explicit common assumptions, joint testing and lifecycle cost.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A quantified/argued diversity case naming which common modes are reduced, which remain shared, adjudication coverage and maintenance/recovery consequences.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Separate teams or languages are not the property; reduced joint failure for identified causes is.

CURRENT_STATUS

  3. SRC-054

  4. SRC-061

EMPIRICAL_OR_FIELD_EVIDENCE

Homogeneous ecosystem updates can propagate broadly (SRC-061, SRC-062), but empirical multiversion studies show diversity cannot be assumed independent (SRC-014).

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

Properly chosen physical, technological or supplier diversity can materially reduce specific common causes.

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How can diversity benefit be measured for modern AI/model/data stacks where many hidden assumptions are shared?

FAILURE_PROPAGATION_PROFILE
APPLICABLE

true

PROPERTY

Design diversity as conditional common-mode mitigation

INITIATING_FAULT_OR_FAILURE

Common specification ambiguity; difficult inputs induce similar errors; shared algorithm/library/data/model; voter/acceptance test wrong; diversity overwhelms maintenance.

LOCAL_EFFECT

Loss, corruption, delay, false indication or degraded performance at the initiating item.

NEXT_LEVEL_EFFECT

Effect on the enclosing function, dependency path or maintenance process.

END_EFFECT

Common specification ambiguity; difficult inputs induce similar errors; shared algorithm/library/data/model; voter/acceptance test wrong; diversity overwhelms maintenance.

PROPAGATION_PATH

Functional, physical, state, timing, control, resource or organisational coupling.

CONTAINMENT_BOUNDARY

A declared fault-containment region or explicit stop/degradation boundary.

DEPENDENCIES

Clear specification/oracle, adjudication, independent causal pathways, configuration control, joint-failure evidence and maintenance capacity.

COMMON_CAUSE_OR_SHARED_COUPLING

Shared specification, environment, supplier, control plane, state, maintenance or operator path must be challenged.

LATENT_CONDITION

Standby, monitoring, repair or dependency defects may remain latent until demand.

DETECTION

Outcome- or function-relevant observation, not a component heartbeat alone.

CONTROL

Diversify selected causal dimensions; analyse shared assumptions; use independent evidence/teams/tools where valuable; test coincident errors; preserve diagnosability and update compatibility.

RESIDUAL_RISK

Unmodelled combinations, dependence, coverage gaps and changed configuration remain.

The specified number/class of faults tolerated while meeting required function or an accepted degraded state.

FAILURE_DOMAINS

Physical, power, network, control-plane, state, software, supplier, operator and maintenance domains.

INDEPENDENCE_EVIDENCE

Evidence must address shared causes rather than infer independence from copy count or labels.

COMMON_CAUSE_COUPLINGS

Specification, design, implementation, environment, supplier, maintenance, configuration and operational coupling.

STANDBY_LATENCY_OR_DORMANCY

Dormant faults, activation delay and cold/warm/hot readiness are measured when applicable.

SWITCHOVER_COVERAGE

DEGRADED_MODE

ERM-P041 — MTBF as a universal scalar for reliability
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P041

PROPERTY_NAME

MTBF as a universal scalar for reliability

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Constant-rate electronic reliability prediction and repairable-system HPP practice.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. HARDWARE_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

  2. LIFE_DATA_AND_STATISTICAL_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. RELIABILITY_ENGINEERING_NATIVE

  2. STATISTICAL_RELIABILITY_ANCESTRY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Represent reliability by mean time between failures, often as the reciprocal of a constant failure rate.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

A convenient scalar is used to compare items and plan tests/spares.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

No standalone required function is protected; MTBF is an estimand only under a defined recurrent-event process and failure definition.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Mean hides mission time, hazard shape, tail, repair, severity, dependence and functional consequence; constant-rate premise is unstated.

MECHANISM

Rejected as a universal property; retain MTBF only as a model-qualified descriptive measure with confidence and exposure.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Only when recurrent failures are reasonably represented by HPP/constant intensity or as an explicitly limited empirical mean.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Report exposure, failure count/intensity, uncertainty and mission reliability directly.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Stable failure definition, repairable-system process, representative exposure, constant-intensity validation if exponential inference is made.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Limited: compact planning/comparison measure inside its valid model.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Test planner, maintenance/logistics analyst or model user—not a generic mission consumer.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

“MTBF 100,000 h” read as life; reciprocal used without constant hazard; mixed failures; no confidence; predicted part rates summed as field truth.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

NIST makes the constant-rate assumption explicit; the National Academies critique documents handbook misuse and inaccurate predictions.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Universal slogan narrowed to one estimand among mission reliability, failure intensity, life distribution and availability measures.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Use only with declared model, failure population, exposure, confidence and decision; never substitute it for mission or field reliability.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

The target/slogan is ceremony when detached from model and mission.

CURRENT_STATUS

CEREMONY_NOT_GENERAL_PROPERTY

MODERATE

INCIDENT_OR_FAILURE_CASE_STRENGTH

MODERATE

OPERATIONAL_PRACTICE_STRENGTH

HIGH

LOW_TO_MODERATE

TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH

LOW_AS_UNIVERSAL

ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY

VERY_HIGH

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

VERY_HIGH

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. SRC-043

  2. SRC-044

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. SRC-041

  2. SRC-046

  3. SRC-047

EMPIRICAL_OR_FIELD_EVIDENCE

Field and incident data show severity and correlated outages can dominate a mean interval.

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

MTBF remains mathematically coherent and operationally useful in some stationary repairable-system contexts.

OPEN_QUESTIONS

What communication formats most reliably prevent non-experts from reading MTBF as expected lifetime?

ERM-P042 — “Five nines” as a universal service-reliability claim
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P042

PROPERTY_NAME

“Five nines” as a universal service-reliability claim

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Telecommunications/high-availability uptime targets and later service marketing/SRE shorthand.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_OR_SRE_TRANSLATION

  2. DOMAIN_SPECIFIC

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_TRANSLATION

  2. DOMAIN_TRANSLATION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Express annual allowed downtime by counting nines of availability.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Communicate a compact availability objective.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

No universal function; validity requires a specific SLI, denominator, scope, consumer, window and exclusions.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Nines hide whether failures are one long outage or many interruptions, which users/regions/journeys count, degraded service, data correctness and correlated dependency loss.

MECHANISM

Reject the slogan as evidence; translate to consumer journeys, outage distribution, tails, consequence and recovery objectives.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Only as shorthand after full metric semantics are already defined and understood.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

State maximum tolerable outage patterns or direct user/mission outcomes instead.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Valid SLI/denominator, complete telemetry, user weighting, interval, planned-maintenance treatment and outage-shape analysis.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Limited communication convenience.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Service stakeholders who already understand the full definition.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Marketing without scope; request-weighting; excluded windows; short-outage accumulation; partial outage hidden; regional correlation.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

USENIX criticism directly questions whether counting nines reflects user experience and notes inability to distinguish outage shapes.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Nines were supplemented by user-focused SLIs, SLOs, burn rates, cohorts, tails and incident consequence.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Never claim reliability from the number of nines alone; publish the underlying outcome distribution and scope.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

The nines badge is the ceremony.

A well-defined availability percentage can be useful for capacity/contract decisions; the universal branding is rejected.
