ERM-P043 — Universal bathtub-curve assumption
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P043

PROPERTY_NAME

Universal bathtub-curve assumption

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Early lifetime/hazard modelling and an influential three-phase graphical idealisation.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. LIFE_DATA_AND_STATISTICAL_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. STATISTICAL_RELIABILITY_ANCESTRY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Assume every product has infant mortality, a constant-hazard useful life, then wear-out.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Provide an intuitive lifecycle story for screening, random failures and replacement.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

No standalone mission property; it is a candidate hazard-shape model.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Observed modes/populations do not exhibit all phases; mixtures create aggregate shape; item changes/maintenance violate stationary phases; policy follows the sketch rather than data/mechanism.

MECHANISM

Reject universality; fit/compare distributions and mechanisms, stratify populations, use repairable-event models where appropriate.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Use only when mechanism and data support the relevant segment for the decision.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Plot empirical hazard/ROCOF with uncertainty or use a conservative nonparametric bound.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Life data with censoring, homogeneous enough population, mechanism knowledge, exposure and model diagnostics.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

Access, modularity, safe isolation, procedures, test equipment, spares, tools, skills, logistics, configuration data and time under actual field conditions.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Limited conceptual aid when its phases actually match the system.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Reliability analyst and maintenance planner.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Forced constant hazard; age replacement without wear-out; burn-in for non-infant modes; population mixture; repairable system treated as lifetime sample.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Peer-reviewed critique and RCM field evidence directly reject universal use.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

A universal lifecycle picture narrowed to one possible hazard pattern among many, with mechanism-specific maintenance.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Treat the bathtub as a hypothesis/teaching model, never an assumed empirical law.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

The curve on a slide is ceremony when it predetermines policy.

CURRENT_STATUS

REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

  3. SRC-042

  4. SRC-051

EMPIRICAL_OR_FIELD_EVIDENCE

Nowlan and Heap’s aircraft component study documented six patterns and set mechanism-specific maintenance conditions (SRC-008).

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

Some physical products genuinely show early defects, a useful-life plateau and wear-out; local use remains valid.

OPEN_QUESTIONS

How often are aggregate bathtub shapes artefacts of heterogeneous cohorts rather than individual mechanisms?

RELIABILITY_EVIDENCE_PROFILE
APPLICABLE

true

PROPERTY

Universal bathtub-curve assumption

CLAIM

Treat the bathtub as a hypothesis/teaching model, never an assumed empirical law.

POPULATION_OR_DENOMINATOR

Declared units, missions, demands, requests, users, time windows or recurrent-event process.

CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY

Exact hardware, software, data, model, dependency and procedure versions.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE

Representative demand/workload/environment with consequence-important rare cases identified.

TEST_OR_OBSERVATION_DESIGN

Predeclared sampling/censoring, failure scoring, exposure and stopping rules.

MODEL

Model selected for the stated estimand: life distribution, recurrent-event process, binomial demand, availability state model or direct functional check.

ASSUMPTIONS

Independence, stationarity, censoring, repair effectiveness, acceleration, coverage and representativeness are explicit.

CONFIDENCE_OR_UNCERTAINTY

Intervals, posterior uncertainty or bounded qualitative confidence; point estimates alone are insufficient.

CENSORING_AND_MISSINGNESS

Right/interval censoring, unreported events, lost telemetry and excluded windows are recorded.

COVERAGE

Claim coverage is limited to tested/observed failure modes, environments and paths.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Reliability analyst and maintenance planner.

CHEAP_PATH

Plot empirical hazard/ROCOF with uncertainty or use a conservative nonparametric bound.

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

Some physical products genuinely show early defects, a useful-life plateau and wear-out; local use remains valid.

EXPIRY_OR_FRESHNESS

Evidence expires when configuration, profile, environment, support organisation or dependencies materially change.

MATURE_FORM

Treat the bathtub as a hypothesis/teaching model, never an assumed empirical law.

DISPOSITION_RATIONALE

Universal form rejected; mechanism- and data-supported local hazard models retained under ERM-P022/ERM-P027/ERM-P028.

ERM-P044 — Handbook reliability prediction as field-reliability proof
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P044

PROPERTY_NAME

Handbook reliability prediction as field-reliability proof

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

RCA and MIL-HDBK-217 component-rate prediction programmes.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. HARDWARE_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

  2. LIFE_DATA_AND_STATISTICAL_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. RELIABILITY_ENGINEERING_NATIVE

  2. STATISTICAL_RELIABILITY_ANCESTRY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Sum adjusted generic component failure rates to predict equipment MTBF before fielding.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Support design comparison, requirements allocation and early planning before sufficient field data exist.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

No direct field function is established by the table; it is a model output for a declared design/environment.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Outdated database, constant-rate form, missing mechanisms, wrong use conditions, deterministic point output and contractual ritual are presented as achieved reliability.

MECHANISM

Reject equivalence; use physics/mechanism and field calibration, uncertainty, sensitivity and comparative purpose only where validated.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Early comparative design or planning when no better evidence exists and limitations are explicit.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Use qualitative stress/margin review, targeted tests and field analogues when a numerical point prediction adds false precision.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Technology/current data, environmental/profile match, mechanism relevance, uncertainty and calibration.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

At most, rough comparison or allocation under controlled assumptions.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Design/trade-study and planning authority—not final field acceptance.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Obsolete parts data; generic quality factors; no failure mechanisms; multiplicative factors; point prediction; inherited contractual target.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

The National Academies appendix reports gross over- and underprediction and concludes the method can mislead.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Generic handbook prediction narrowed/replaced by objectives-driven design, physics of failure, empirical field updating and uncertainty.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Prediction is labelled model-based, comparative and uncertain; field/mission reliability requires representative evidence.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

A handbook calculation is ceremony when performed to satisfy a deliverable rather than inform a decision.

CURRENT_STATUS

EMPIRICAL_OR_FIELD_EVIDENCE

Technology and use-condition changes documented by the National Academies undermine direct transfer of old generic rates (SRC-047).

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

Some calibrated prediction tools can rank design alternatives; the rejected claim is equality with field truth.

OPEN_QUESTIONS

What validation criteria are sufficient before a modern prediction model can support absolute rather than comparative claims?

ERM-P045 — Completed FMEA/FMECA as proof that failure modes are controlled
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P045

PROPERTY_NAME

Completed FMEA/FMECA as proof that failure modes are controlled

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Institutionalised FMEA/FMECA deliverables in military, aerospace, automotive and other programmes.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. FAILURE_ANALYSIS_LINEAGE

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. RELIABILITY_ENGINEERING_NATIVE

  2. SAFETY_IMPORT_OR_SHARED_ANCESTRY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Complete and approve a worksheet listing failure modes, effects, criticality and controls.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Create systematic visibility and prioritisation of plausible failure modes.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

No mission protection follows from completion alone; each important mode needs effective prevention/detection/containment/recovery or accepted residual risk.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Copied modes; subjective ranks; missing interactions/software/human factors; “control” untested; document stale; rows closed by wording.

MECHANISM

Reject completion claim; retain ERM-P006 systematic challenge and bind material rows to design/test/field closure.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Use a structured FMEA when bottom-up functional/component analysis is decision-relevant and proportionate.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Focused failure challenge or fault tree for the live top event when a full table adds no value.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Current design, competent participants, scope/ground rules, interface/dependence analysis and closure evidence.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

The method can find modes; completion itself has no independent payoff.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Design, reliability, maintenance and acceptance teams.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Checkbox culture; RPN arithmetic; row inflation; stale revision; controls without coverage; multiple-failure blindness.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Method limitations and bureaucracy are well documented; modern standards support tailoring rather than universal sufficiency.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Worksheet completion narrowed to objective-driven, cross-method, evidence-linked failure analysis.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Only claim that defined failure modes were systematically challenged and specific controls verified; never “FMEA complete, therefore reliable.”

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

The approved worksheet is the ceremony; ERM-P006 is the property.

CURRENT_STATUS

CEREMONY_NOT_GENERAL_PROPERTY

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH

HIGH

MODERATE

MODERATE

HIGH

HIGH_FOR_CRITIQUE

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH

PRIMARY_SOURCES

  1. SRC-004

  2. SRC-035

CRITICAL_SOURCES

  1. SRC-005

  2. SRC-079

  3. SRC-058

  4. SRC-060

EMPIRICAL_OR_FIELD_EVIDENCE

Complex outages involve interacting change, dependency, state and recovery mechanisms that part-by-part completion can omit.

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

Well-executed FMEA remains useful and widely retained; only sufficiency-by-completion is rejected.

OPEN_QUESTIONS

Which observable indicators distinguish a live, decision-effective FMEA from a compliant but inert one?

MISSION_RELIABILITY_PROFILE
APPLICABLE

false

PROFILE_TYPE

MISSION_RELIABILITY_PROFILE

REASON

The candidate is a supporting mechanism or rejected proxy rather than a standalone mission claim.

FAILURE_PROPAGATION_PROFILE
APPLICABLE

true

PROPERTY

Completed FMEA/FMECA as proof that failure modes are controlled

INITIATING_FAULT_OR_FAILURE

Copied modes; subjective ranks; missing interactions/software/human factors; “control” untested; document stale; rows closed by wording.

LOCAL_EFFECT

Loss, corruption, delay, false indication or degraded performance at the initiating item.

NEXT_LEVEL_EFFECT

Effect on the enclosing function, dependency path or maintenance process.

END_EFFECT

Copied modes; subjective ranks; missing interactions/software/human factors; “control” untested; document stale; rows closed by wording.

PROPAGATION_PATH

Functional, physical, state, timing, control, resource or organisational coupling.

CONTAINMENT_BOUNDARY

A declared fault-containment region or explicit stop/degradation boundary.

DEPENDENCIES

Current design, competent participants, scope/ground rules, interface/dependence analysis and closure evidence.

COMMON_CAUSE_OR_SHARED_COUPLING

Shared specification, environment, supplier, control plane, state, maintenance or operator path must be challenged.

LATENT_CONDITION

Standby, monitoring, repair or dependency defects may remain latent until demand.

DETECTION

Outcome- or function-relevant observation, not a component heartbeat alone.

CONTROL

Reject completion claim; retain ERM-P006 systematic challenge and bind material rows to design/test/field closure.

RESIDUAL_RISK

Unmodelled combinations, dependence, coverage gaps and changed configuration remain.

false

ERM-P046 — Backup existence as proof that recovery is solved
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P046

PROPERTY_NAME

Backup existence as proof that recovery is solved

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Backup and contingency-plan compliance.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. AVAILABILITY_AND_REPAIRABLE_SYSTEMS_LINEAGE

  2. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_OR_SRE_TRANSLATION

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. MAINTAINABILITY_NATIVE

  2. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_TRANSLATION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Verify that backup jobs run and copies exist.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Preserve data/artefacts for loss scenarios.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Restored, authoritative and functionally usable state within RPO/RTO—not copy existence.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Stale/corrupt/inaccessible/incompatible backup; missing key/tool; incomplete application state; same disaster affects source; restore never tested.

MECHANISM

Reject existence claim; apply ERM-P017 recurring restore/reconstitution validation.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Never sufficient alone; backup is one precondition in a recovery case.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For reproducible stateless assets, verify build-from-source and one end-to-end check.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Integrity, freshness, isolation, tooling, configuration, capacity, procedures, state semantics and trained staff.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

A valid recovery source, authorised action, state/data reconciliation, dependency readiness and independently checked return-to-service postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Copy existence has potential value only when the complete recovery path is valid.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Data/service owner and continuity authority.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Green job; empty archive; partial DB; wrong version; inaccessible credentials; unbounded restore time.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Incident evidence directly refutes the equivalence.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Backup compliance evolved toward restore exercises, functional validation and reconstitution.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Use ERM-P017; do not claim recoverability from inventory alone.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Backup-count dashboard is the ceremony.

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

None affecting disposition; optimal exercise frequency remains context-specific.

true

PROPERTY

Backup existence as proof that recovery is solved

INITIATING_FAILURE

Stale/corrupt/inaccessible/incompatible backup; missing key/tool; incomplete application state; same disaster affects source; restore never tested.

RECOVERY_ACTION

Reject existence claim; apply ERM-P017 recurring restore/reconstitution validation.

AUTHORITY_SOURCE

The designated current configuration/state authority and authorised operator or controller.

CHECKPOINT_BACKUP_SPARE_OR_ALTERNATE

Declared checkpoint, replica, backup, spare, rebuild source or manual workaround.

RPO_OR_STATE_LOSS_BOUND

Explicit allowed state/data loss or “none” for state-continuous functions.

RTO_OR_RESTORATION_BOUND

Explicit time-to-restoration bound including diagnosis, logistics, repair and validation.

STATE_RECONCILIATION

Resolve divergent, stale, partial or replayed state before authoritative service resumes.

FUNCTIONAL_POSTCONDITIONS

Restored, authoritative and functionally usable state within RPO/RTO—not copy existence.

DATA_STATE_POSTCONDITIONS

Integrity, completeness, freshness, ordering and authority appropriate to the mission.

Critical dependencies and capacity are verified rather than assumed from local restart.

Independent end-to-end functional and state checks under representative demand.

ROLLBACK_OR_FALLBACK

PROPERTY

ERM-P047 — One failover test as proof of disaster recovery
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P047

PROPERTY_NAME

One failover test as proof of disaster recovery

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

High-availability acceptance and disaster-recovery exercises.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. AVAILABILITY_AND_REPAIRABLE_SYSTEMS_LINEAGE

  2. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_OR_SRE_TRANSLATION

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. FAULT_TOLERANCE_IMPORT_OR_SHARED_ANCESTRY

  2. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_TRANSLATION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Trigger failover once and record that the secondary became active.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Demonstrate transfer capability.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Recovery across the relevant disaster/fault population, current configuration, representative state/load and postconditions.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Only nominal fault; shared control survives; secondary warm; no state divergence; no user journey; no return/failback; stale evidence after change.

MECHANISM

Reject generalisation; maintain scenario/claim coverage, current configuration, correlated faults, load/state, recovery/failback and postcondition evidence.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

A failover test is one evidence item when it discriminates a live claim.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Directly verify the changed component/path after small changes; reserve full exercises for material uncertainties.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

ERM-P008/009, current state, representative fault/load, independent observability, abort and recovery validation.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

Credible failure-domain independence, common-cause analysis, standby condition, switchover coverage and state compatibility.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

A valid recovery source, authorised action, state/data reconciliation, dependency readiness and independently checked return-to-service postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

A well-designed test supports the tested scenario only.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Continuity/service owner and acceptance authority.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Happy-path failover; no latent standby; control plane excluded; no load; state loss hidden; test environment unlike field.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Meta’s drills helped, but the actual global-backbone failure was outside exercised coverage.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Annual DR ritual evolved toward claim-based, configuration-aware scenario portfolios and continuous cheaper checks.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

State exactly which fault/state/load/postconditions the exercise established and which remain untested.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

The annual pass certificate is the ceremony.

ERM-P048 — No incidents or lower incident count as proof of reliability improvement
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P048

PROPERTY_NAME

No incidents or lower incident count as proof of reliability improvement

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Failure-count/growth tracking and modern incident metrics.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_OR_SRE_TRANSLATION

  2. RELIABILITY_GROWTH_LINEAGE

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_TRANSLATION

  2. RELIABILITY_ENGINEERING_NATIVE

ORIGINAL_FORM

Use fewer failures/incidents over time as evidence of improvement.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Provide a simple trend signal.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

No standalone function is established without exposure, severity, detection/reporting and configuration/profile context.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Lower demand; hidden/uncaught incidents; severity reclassification; fewer but larger correlated outages; reporting suppression; changed denominator.

MECHANISM

Reject raw count; normalise by exposure/profile, stratify consequence and recurrence family, track latent/near-miss evidence and detection changes.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Use incident rates only with stable definitions/exposure and as one part of ERM-P024/025/028 evidence.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

For low counts, inspect each failure mechanism and consequence rather than model a trend.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Event definition, exposure, detection/reporting consistency, severity, configuration and recurrence families.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Limited signal when denominators and ascertainment are stable.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Reliability/service owner and decision authority.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Goodhart effects; ticket splitting/merging; reporting fear; observation changes; tail hidden by count.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Incident absence is compatible with latent failure, low exposure or weak detection.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Raw counts evolved toward exposure-normalised rates, user impact, recurrence and causal corrective-action evidence.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Treat count as descriptive; claim improvement only with stable exposure, detection and mechanism/impact evidence.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

Incident-count OKR is the ceremony.

ERM-P049 — Green health check as proof that required function is available
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P049

PROPERTY_NAME

Green health check as proof that required function is available

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Built-in test and automated liveness/readiness endpoints.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. DIAGNOSTICS_AND_PHM_LINEAGE

  2. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_OR_SRE_TRANSLATION

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. MAINTAINABILITY_NATIVE

  2. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_TRANSLATION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Use a green heartbeat or health endpoint to mark an item available.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Enable rapid automated routing and status display.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

End-to-end required function with correct state, dependencies and performance.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Probe checks process existence/local loop; dependency/user path fails; monitor stale; partial/cohort failure; latent standby absent.

MECHANISM

Reject equivalence; apply ERM-P011/ERM-P033 layered coverage and end-to-end functional checks.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

A health bit can govern local routing only for the failure modes it demonstrably covers.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Execute a real safe user/mission transaction.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Coverage model, representative path, independent monitor and state correctness.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Enumerated fault/failure population, observable signals, calibrated thresholds, isolation resolution, false-alarm and missed-detection costs.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Fast local indication within bounded coverage.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Traffic manager/operator; not a generic mission claimant.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

False green, false red, cached response, monitor shares failure, healthy component unreachable.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Component alive is not system function; incident evidence is direct.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

One-bit health evolved toward layered symptoms, journeys, state and diagnostic coverage.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

A green signal is labelled with exact coverage and never expanded beyond it.

Meta reported DNS servers remained operational but became unreachable, directly refuting local-green-equals-service-available (SRC-059).

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

None affecting disposition; coverage maintenance remains an ongoing question.

ERM-P050 — Chaos exercise as general proof of resilience
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P050

PROPERTY_NAME

Chaos exercise as general proof of resilience

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Popularisation and branding of chaos engineering.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. FAULT_INJECTION_OR_CHAOS_TRANSLATION

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. DOMAIN_TRANSLATION

  2. FAULT_TOLERANCE_IMPORT_OR_SHARED_ANCESTRY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Run a fault-injection event and conclude the system is resilient.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Challenge integrated failure handling.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

No general resilience property follows; evidence is bounded to the hypothesis, fault, profile, boundary, observation and recovery postconditions.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Toy fault; easy steady-state metric; no state/data validation; unrepresentative load; no correlated failure; no action; survivorship bias.

MECHANISM

Reject ceremony; apply ERM-P035 hypothesis-bound experiments selected by decision value.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Only when a live uncertainty cannot be resolved more cheaply/safely and experiment controls are adequate.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Static analysis, simulation, staging or direct functional/recovery test.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Hypothesis, coverage, containment, observability, abort, representative state/load and decision consumer.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Specific evidence for the tested claim.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Service/mission owner and reliability decision-maker.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Chaos theatre; production harm; impressive demo; repeated schedule; no denominator; no postconditions.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

The 2025 multivocal review identifies unresolved evaluation/adoption gaps; principles are advocacy, not independent proof.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Random breakage/“monkey” branding narrowed to controlled, hypothesis-driven, decision-linked experiments.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Never claim general resilience; publish tested claim, boundaries, observations, recovery validation and resulting decision.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

The named chaos event is the ceremony.

ERM-P051 — More redundancy always improves reliability
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P051

PROPERTY_NAME

More redundancy always improves reliability

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Parallel RBD arithmetic and fault-tolerance intuition.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. HARDWARE_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

  2. FAULT_TOLERANT_COMPUTING_LINEAGE

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. RELIABILITY_ENGINEERING_NATIVE

  2. FAULT_TOLERANCE_IMPORT_OR_SHARED_ANCESTRY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Add copies/paths so independent joint failure becomes less likely.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Reduce random component failure probability.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Improved mission outcome after specified faults, not increased component count.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Common cause, voter/switch fault, dormant spare, state split, capacity/overload, maintenance complexity, update concentration or new interfaces dominate.

MECHANISM

Reject monotonic slogan; apply ERM-P008/009/040 and compare total system reliability/maintainability under dependence and coverage.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Add redundancy only when failure modes, domains, coverage, state and lifecycle burden support positive net value.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Simplify/eliminate the failure cause, add a manual spare, improve repair, or accept risk when redundancy burden exceeds benefit.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Common-cause model, transfer coverage, capacity/state, maintenance/support and evidence.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

Credible failure-domain independence, common-cause analysis, standby condition, switchover coverage and state compatibility.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Can be large under valid independence; can be negative otherwise.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Architect and mission/risk authority.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Shared power/control/specification; hidden standby; complexity; simultaneous maintenance; false failover; correlated release.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Formal and empirical literature directly show dependence can destroy the multiplicative benefit.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Copy-count reasoning evolved toward failure-domain, coverage, diversity, state and supportability cases.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

No redundancy claim without a total-system conditional argument and cheaper-path comparison.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

The N+1/TMR diagram is the ceremony when assumptions are unstated.

CONTAINMENT_BOUNDARY

A declared fault-containment region or explicit stop/degradation boundary.

DEPENDENCIES

Sensors, data, transfer, uncertainty, lead time, intervention efficacy and loss comparison.

COMMON_CAUSE_OR_SHARED_COUPLING

Shared specification, environment, supplier, control plane, state, maintenance or operator path must be challenged.

LATENT_CONDITION

Standby, monitoring, repair or dependency defects may remain latent until demand.

DETECTION

Outcome- or function-relevant observation, not a component heartbeat alone.

CONTROL

Reject universality; use ERM-P023 comparative, uncertainty-aware, mechanism- and decision-valid assessment.

RESIDUAL_RISK

Unmodelled combinations, dependence, coverage gaps and changed configuration remain.

REDUNDANCY_COMMON_CAUSE_PROFILE
APPLICABLE

false

PROFILE_TYPE

REDUNDANCY_COMMON_CAUSE_PROFILE

REASON

The property does not itself claim redundancy benefit; any redundant implementation must invoke ERM-P008/ERM-P009.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PROFILE
APPLICABLE

true

PROPERTY

Predictive maintenance universally outperforms simpler policies

TARGET_FAULTS_FAILURES

No precursor; weak labels; domain shift; false alarms; late warning; costly sensor stack; maintenance action itself harmful; benchmark not prospective.

OBSERVABLES

Function outputs, timing, state, residuals, dependency signals and user/mission outcomes.

DETECTION_COVERAGE

Coverage is stated for an enumerated fault/failure population, operating mode and latency window.

FALSE_ALARM_COST

Unnecessary maintenance, failover, shutdown, operator load or customer disruption.

MISSED_DETECTION_COST

Continued propagation, latent damage, missed recovery window or false assurance.

ISOLATION_RESOLUTION

Replaceable unit, functional region, dependency or ambiguity group appropriate to the action.

AMBIGUITY_GROUPS

Plausible causes producing the same symptom are preserved until discriminated.

LATENCY

Detection and isolation time are bounded relative to propagation and recovery deadlines.

CONFIDENCE_CALIBRATION

Uncertainty, thresholds, drift and out-of-distribution conditions are exposed.

OPERATOR_OR_AUTOMATION_ACTION

Action is authority-bounded, reversible where feasible and matched to diagnosis confidence.

RECOVERY_COVERAGE

A detected fault counts only if the prescribed action restores or contains the target failure with known coverage.

MATURE_FORM

No “PdM is better” claim without prospective comparison to the cheapest credible policy.

false

PROFILE_TYPE

RELIABILITY_EVIDENCE_PROFILE
APPLICABLE

true

PROPERTY

Predictive maintenance universally outperforms simpler policies

CLAIM

No “PdM is better” claim without prospective comparison to the cheapest credible policy.

POPULATION_OR_DENOMINATOR

Declared units, missions, demands, requests, users, time windows or recurrent-event process.

CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY

Exact hardware, software, data, model, dependency and procedure versions.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE

Representative demand/workload/environment with consequence-important rare cases identified.

TEST_OR_OBSERVATION_DESIGN

Predeclared sampling/censoring, failure scoring, exposure and stopping rules.

MODEL

Model selected for the stated estimand: life distribution, recurrent-event process, binomial demand, availability state model or direct functional check.

ASSUMPTIONS

Independence, stationarity, censoring, repair effectiveness, acceleration, coverage and representativeness are explicit.

CONFIDENCE_OR_UNCERTAINTY

Intervals, posterior uncertainty or bounded qualitative confidence; point estimates alone are insufficient.

CENSORING_AND_MISSINGNESS

Right/interval censoring, unreported events, lost telemetry and excluded windows are recorded.

COVERAGE

Claim coverage is limited to tested/observed failure modes, environments and paths.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Asset owner and maintenance planner.

CHEAP_PATH

Run-to-failure, inspection or scheduled maintenance when net performance is comparable.

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

Specific well-instrumented degradation mechanisms support effective PdM.

EXPIRY_OR_FRESHNESS

Evidence expires when configuration, profile, environment, support organisation or dependencies materially change.

MATURE_FORM

No “PdM is better” claim without prospective comparison to the cheapest credible policy.

DISPOSITION_RATIONALE

Universal superiority rejected; conditional PHM property retained as ERM-P023.

ERM-P053 — Reliability-growth curve alone proves causal design improvement
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P053

PROPERTY_NAME

Reliability-growth curve alone proves causal design improvement

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Duane/Crow-AMSAA tracking and software growth models.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. RELIABILITY_GROWTH_LINEAGE

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. RELIABILITY_ENGINEERING_NATIVE

  2. STATISTICAL_RELIABILITY_ANCESTRY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Infer improvement from a declining failure intensity or increasing MTBF curve over test time.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Track whether reliability appears to improve during development.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Verified reduction of relevant failure mechanisms under representative exposure.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Test selection changes; easy failures fixed first; delayed fixes; censoring; profile shifts; selective exclusion; model fit mistaken for cause.

MECHANISM

Reject curve-only claim; use ERM-P024/025 with causal fix records, configuration, representative retest and recurrence evidence.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Use growth plots as monitoring/model tools, not standalone causal proof.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

List material failure families, fixes and targeted regression outcomes when sample is small.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Stable scoring/exposure, model fit, fix history, configuration, independent evaluation and representative profile.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Trend visualisation and planning within assumptions.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Reliability programme and decision authority.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Post-hoc fit; selected endpoints; unimplemented fix; perfect repair assumption; test maturity mistaken for design improvement.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

National Academies review and handbook tailoring stress that growth requires management/test/fix conditions, not curve shape alone.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Curve tracking evolved toward planned fix effectiveness, delayed-fix models, recurrence and evidence closure.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Report the curve as one model plus explicit causal interventions and uncertainty.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

The improving chart is the ceremony.

Under controlled stable testing with known fixes, growth models are useful for estimation and planning.

How should growth claims separate learning from test-profile drift in continuous deployment?

ERM-P054 — Zero observed failures means zero failure probability or an unlimited reliability claim
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P054

PROPERTY_NAME

Zero observed failures means zero failure probability or an unlimited reliability claim

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

Success-run and zero-failure reliability demonstrations.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. LIFE_DATA_AND_STATISTICAL_RELIABILITY_LINEAGE

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. STATISTICAL_RELIABILITY_ANCESTRY

ORIGINAL_FORM

Accept a reliability target after a specified number of failure-free trials/hours.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Demonstrate a lower reliability bound when failures are rare.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

A bounded reliability statement at specified confidence for a population/profile/model.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

No confidence/denominator; dependent trials; right-censoring ignored; unrepresentative exposure; lifetime shape inferred without failures.

MECHANISM

Reject zero-probability inference; compute the valid bound under assumptions and disclose inability to identify failure-time behaviour.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Use zero-failure plans only when trial semantics, independence/dependence and claim are explicit.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

State “no failures observed in N representative demands/hours” plus the appropriate bound; do not overmodel.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Sample/population, exposure, independence or dependence model, censoring, failure oracle and confidence.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Observable functional outcomes sufficient to determine whether the claimed property actually holds.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

No recovery premise is inherent; any claim that depends on restoration must invoke explicit recovery postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Can provide meaningful lower-bound evidence despite no observed failures.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Acceptance/test authority.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Rule of three misapplied; unit-hours pooled; repeated demands dependent; success selection; no tail information.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

Primary statistical sources directly distinguish a minimum bound from zero risk or a life distribution.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

Zero-failure pass/fail evolved toward explicit confidence, dependence and censoring treatment.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Publish exact test design and bounded inference; never “zero failures therefore reliable.”

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

The zero-failure badge/certificate is the ceremony.

ERM-P055 — AI/ML/agentic diagnosis or auto-remediation as a general reliability property
PROPERTY_ID

ERM-P055

PROPERTY_NAME

AI/ML/agentic diagnosis or auto-remediation as a general reliability property

HISTORICAL_ORIGIN

ML anomaly detection/PHM, AIOps, autonomous repair and rapidly emerging agentic systems.

LINEAGE_CLASS

  1. DOMAIN_SPECIFIC

  2. DIAGNOSTICS_AND_PHM_LINEAGE

  3. SERVICE_RELIABILITY_OR_SRE_TRANSLATION

  4. ONLY_ANALOGOUS

RELATIONSHIP_TO_OTHER_TRADITIONS

  1. DOMAIN_TRANSLATION

  2. ONLY_ANALOGOUS

  3. UNRESOLVED_TENSION

ORIGINAL_FORM

Use learned models/agents to detect anomalies, diagnose causes, predict failure and autonomously remediate.

PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED

Complex systems produce more telemetry and faster events than humans can always interpret or act on.

REQUIRED_FUNCTION_OR_MISSION

Improve detection/restoration outcomes for a defined function without introducing unacceptable nondeterminism, drift, authority or cascading risk.

FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT

Distribution drift; uncalibrated confidence; opaque cause; tool/action hallucination; shared model/data dependency; unsafe authority; repeated remediation loop; evidence destruction.

MECHANISM

Bound use case/action authority; monitor postdeployment drift; expose uncertainty/abstention; require independent functional postconditions; sandbox/canary/reversible actions; human escalation and non-AI fallback.

TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT

Narrow, observable tasks with representative evaluation, reversible bounded action and independent verification where automation value exceeds risk.

NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH

Rules, deterministic functional checks, manual diagnosis or conventional automation when they resolve the same task more transparently.

DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS

Current data/profile, trusted telemetry, calibration, drift detection, action sandbox, configuration/state authority, recovery fallback and governance.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE_PRECONDITIONS

A declared and sufficiently representative operating profile, environment, duty cycle, mission phase and maintenance state.

FAILURE_MODEL_PRECONDITIONS

A failure model appropriate to the estimand; distinguish non-repairable life, recurrent repairable events, demand failures, software activation and service outcomes.

REDUNDANCY_OR_COMMON_CAUSE_PRECONDITIONS

No redundancy premise is needed; if redundancy appears in the implementation, common-cause and switchover assumptions must be analysed separately.

DIAGNOSTIC_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS

Enumerated fault/failure population, observable signals, calibrated thresholds, isolation resolution, false-alarm and missed-detection costs.

RECOVERY_OR_REPAIR_PRECONDITIONS

A valid recovery source, authorised action, state/data reconciliation, dependency readiness and independently checked return-to-service postconditions.

MAINTAINABILITY_PRECONDITIONS

The design and support system must not make verification or corrective action infeasible.

EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF

Potential speed/scale improvement in bounded diagnostics or remediation; no general field-reliability claim is established.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Operator, maintainer, service/mission owner and automation authority.

KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES

Nondeterminism; brittleness; hidden shared assumptions; out-of-distribution faults; false confidence; prompt/tool injection as boundary; action compounding; accountability gap.

IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS

NIST 2024/2026 sources describe assurance, coverage, monitoring and validation methods as active challenges; evidence is nascent and use-case specific.

HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED

ML prediction expanded toward deployed monitoring and agents, while assurance shifted toward lifecycle monitoring, uncertainty, authority limits and independent validation.

MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM

Only a domain-specific assistive/automated mechanism with bounded function, calibrated uncertainty, drift monitoring, reversible authority, independent postconditions and fallback.

CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY

“AI-powered reliability” branding or an agent demo is not the property; demonstrated bounded net dependability is.

CURRENT_STATUS

DOMAIN_SPECIFIC

EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH

LOW_TO_MODERATE

FORMAL_OR_PROBABILISTIC_STRENGTH

LOW_TO_MIXED

MODERATE

LOW

TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH

LOW

Current authoritative sources establish monitoring/assurance challenges; no broad multi-domain evidence establishes autonomous agents as a general reliability improvement.

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

Narrow anomaly detection, prognostics and automated repair can outperform manual/rule baselines in specific validated settings.

PROPERTY

AI/ML/agentic diagnosis or auto-remediation as a general reliability property

TARGET_FAULTS_FAILURES

Distribution drift; uncalibrated confidence; opaque cause; tool/action hallucination; shared model/data dependency; unsafe authority; repeated remediation loop; evidence destruction.

OBSERVABLES

Function outputs, timing, state, residuals, dependency signals and user/mission outcomes.

DETECTION_COVERAGE

Coverage is stated for an enumerated fault/failure population, operating mode and latency window.

FALSE_ALARM_COST

Unnecessary maintenance, failover, shutdown, operator load or customer disruption.

MISSED_DETECTION_COST

Continued propagation, latent damage, missed recovery window or false assurance.

ISOLATION_RESOLUTION

Replaceable unit, functional region, dependency or ambiguity group appropriate to the action.

AMBIGUITY_GROUPS

Plausible causes producing the same symptom are preserved until discriminated.

LATENCY

Detection and isolation time are bounded relative to propagation and recovery deadlines.

CONFIDENCE_CALIBRATION

Uncertainty, thresholds, drift and out-of-distribution conditions are exposed.

OPERATOR_OR_AUTOMATION_ACTION

Action is authority-bounded, reversible where feasible and matched to diagnosis confidence.

RECOVERY_COVERAGE

A detected fault counts only if the prescribed action restores or contains the target failure with known coverage.

MATURE_FORM

Only a domain-specific assistive/automated mechanism with bounded function, calibrated uncertainty, drift monitoring, reversible authority, independent postconditions and fallback.

RECOVERY_RESTORATION_PROFILE
APPLICABLE

true

PROPERTY

AI/ML/agentic diagnosis or auto-remediation as a general reliability property

INITIATING_FAILURE

Distribution drift; uncalibrated confidence; opaque cause; tool/action hallucination; shared model/data dependency; unsafe authority; repeated remediation loop; evidence destruction.

RECOVERY_ACTION

Bound use case/action authority; monitor postdeployment drift; expose uncertainty/abstention; require independent functional postconditions; sandbox/canary/reversible actions; human escalation and non-AI fallback.

AUTHORITY_SOURCE

The designated current configuration/state authority and authorised operator or controller.

CHECKPOINT_BACKUP_SPARE_OR_ALTERNATE

Declared checkpoint, replica, backup, spare, rebuild source or manual workaround.

RPO_OR_STATE_LOSS_BOUND

Explicit allowed state/data loss or “none” for state-continuous functions.

RTO_OR_RESTORATION_BOUND

Explicit time-to-restoration bound including diagnosis, logistics, repair and validation.

STATE_RECONCILIATION

Resolve divergent, stale, partial or replayed state before authoritative service resumes.

FUNCTIONAL_POSTCONDITIONS

Improve detection/restoration outcomes for a defined function without introducing unacceptable nondeterminism, drift, authority or cascading risk.

DATA_STATE_POSTCONDITIONS

Integrity, completeness, freshness, ordering and authority appropriate to the mission.

DEPENDENCY_POSTCONDITIONS

Critical dependencies and capacity are verified rather than assumed from local restart.

VALIDATION

Independent end-to-end functional and state checks under representative demand.

RETURN_TO_SERVICE_AUTHORITY

Named human or automated authority with explicit acceptance criteria.

ROLLBACK_OR_FALLBACK

A bounded fallback when restoration validation fails or the original cause persists.

MATURE_FORM

Only a domain-specific assistive/automated mechanism with bounded function, calibrated uncertainty, drift monitoring, reversible authority, independent postconditions and fallback.

RELIABILITY_EVIDENCE_PROFILE
APPLICABLE

true

PROPERTY

AI/ML/agentic diagnosis or auto-remediation as a general reliability property

CLAIM

Only a domain-specific assistive/automated mechanism with bounded function, calibrated uncertainty, drift monitoring, reversible authority, independent postconditions and fallback.

POPULATION_OR_DENOMINATOR

Declared units, missions, demands, requests, users, time windows or recurrent-event process.

CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY

Exact hardware, software, data, model, dependency and procedure versions.

OPERATIONAL_PROFILE

Representative demand/workload/environment with consequence-important rare cases identified.

TEST_OR_OBSERVATION_DESIGN

Predeclared sampling/censoring, failure scoring, exposure and stopping rules.

MODEL

Model selected for the stated estimand: life distribution, recurrent-event process, binomial demand, availability state model or direct functional check.

ASSUMPTIONS

Independence, stationarity, censoring, repair effectiveness, acceleration, coverage and representativeness are explicit.

CONFIDENCE_OR_UNCERTAINTY

Intervals, posterior uncertainty or bounded qualitative confidence; point estimates alone are insufficient.

CENSORING_AND_MISSINGNESS

Right/interval censoring, unreported events, lost telemetry and excluded windows are recorded.

COVERAGE

Claim coverage is limited to tested/observed failure modes, environments and paths.

DECISION_OR_CONSUMER

Operator, maintainer, service/mission owner and automation authority.

CHEAP_PATH

Rules, deterministic functional checks, manual diagnosis or conventional automation when they resolve the same task more transparently.

CONTRARY_EVIDENCE

Narrow anomaly detection, prognostics and automated repair can outperform manual/rule baselines in specific validated settings.

EXPIRY_OR_FRESHNESS

Evidence expires when configuration, profile, environment, support organisation or dependencies materially change.

MATURE_FORM

Only a domain-specific assistive/automated mechanism with bounded function, calibrated uncertainty, drift monitoring, reversible authority, independent postconditions and fallback.

DISPOSITION_RATIONALE

No general property admitted. A narrow, independently validated, authority-bounded domain translation remains context-specific and contested.
